# Medals
Aliases: Bronze Medal, Gold Medal, Gold medal, Medal, Silver Medal
Categories: Currencies, Farming
[Medals]
Type: [[Currency]]
Medals are a [[Currency]] earned from [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]], which can be exchanged for various items at [[Anita]]. They are not physical items and cannot be traded: they can only be interacted with in [[Anita]]'s shop.
## Obtaining
### Jacob's Farming Contest
Medals can be earned by participating in [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]]. By the end of the contest, the player has to be in a certain percentage of top players in the contest to earn a medal.
|Bracket|Medal|Top % required|
|-|-|-|
|Bronze Bracket|Bronze Medal|60%|
|Silver Bracket|Silver Medal|30%|
|Gold Bracket|Gold Medal|10%|
|Platinum Bracket|Gold Medal; Bronze Medal|5%|
|Diamond Bracket|Gold Medal; Silver Medal|2%|
### Exchanging
Lower tier Medals can be exchanged to higher tier ones, with each medal being worth the following:
|Medal|Value|
|-|-|
|Silver Medal|[[Medals]] 2 Bronze Medals|
|Gold Medal|[[Medals]] 4 Silver Medals|
## Usage
### [[Anita|Anita's]] Shop
Medals can be used to purchase various items from Anita's shop.
|Item|Cost|
|-|-|
|4 [[Prismapump]]|[[Medals]] 1 Bronze Medal 2x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|[[Hoe of Great Tilling]]|[[Medals]] 1 Bronze Medal 5x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|[[Hoe of Greater Tilling]]|[[Medals]] 1 Silver Medal 10x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|[[Hoe of Greatest Tilling]]|[[Medals]] 2 Silver Medals 20x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|[[Basket of Seeds]]|[[Medals]] 2 Silver Medals 30x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|

|Mechamind Chip ([[Garden Chips]])|[[Medals]] 1 Gold Medal|
|Overdrive Chip ([[Garden Chips]])|[[Medals]] 2 Gold Medals|

Each Medal can be exchanged through [[Anita]] for the following:

|-|-|

|Silver Medal|4 [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|Gold Medal|16 [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
## Tips
- Selling medals by exchanging them to Jacob's Tickets and selling the tickets to the Bazaar is not worth it. Players should use medals to purchase Chips at [[Anita]] and sell the chips to others for more. They can be traded directly, or sold at the Bazaar.

# Motes
Aliases: Mote
Categories: Currencies, Rift Dimension
[Motes]
Motes are the primary [[Currency]] used in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They are used to purchase various items from many [[NPC|NPCs]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
Motes can be obtained by coming in contact with pink orb particles that spawn everywhere in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] and grant 25 Motes and +2 [[Rift Time]]. Motes in [[Enigma's Crib]] grant 10 Motes and no [[Rift Time]].
Motes can also be obtained by killing [[Mob|Mobs]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] and selling items to the [[Motes Grubber]].
Other sources of Motes include:
- A one-time reward of 60,000 Motes, which can be claimed from [[Dr. Emmett]] upon completion of the [[Mirrorverse]].
- [[Ubik's Cube]], which can be used to play Split or Steal with any player or NPC.
Consuming [[McGrubber's Burger|McGrubber's Burgers]] increases the number of Motes granted from orbs and from selling items to [[Motes Grubber]].
## Strategy
- Players joining the [[Rift Dimension]] for the first time can fight [[Leech Supreme]] for 3,000 Motes per boss.
- After obtaining the [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]], [[Autonull|Autonulls]] in the [[Living Cave]] grant 400 Motes and [[Autoboots]] 500 Motes.
- After obtaining the [[Living Timecharm]], [[Bacte]] grants 6,000 Motes per boss.
- After obtaining the [[Globulate Timecharm]] and completing the quest from [[Reed]] to purchase the [[Rift Boat]], [[Thrall|Thralls]] and [[Fledgling|Fledglings]] in the [[Stillgore Château]] can be targeted to craft [[Vampiric Melon|Vampiric Melons]] into [[Enchanted Vampiric Melon|Enchanted Vampiric Melons]] (200 Motes per mob and 21,000 Motes per [[Enchanted Vampiric Melon]]).
  - If the [[Enchanted Vampiric Melon|Enchanted Vampiric Melons]] are sold, each [[Thrall]] or [[Fledgling]] effectively grants 331.25 Motes.
- After obtaining the [[Vampiric Timecharm]] and reaching [[Timite|Timite IV (4)]], [[Highlite]] can be crafted with 32 [[Youngite]], 32 [[Timite]] and 16 [[Obsolite]]. It can then be sold to the [[Motes Grubber]] for 25,000 Motes.
- Every 2hr, the [[Ubik's Cube]] can be used to challenge anyone to a round of Split or Steal. The rightmost [[NPC]] to [[Ubik Von Neumann]] has a 100% chance to split, earning between 25,000 Motes to 45,000 Motes depending on the number of rounds.
## Trivia
- Motes may be a reference to XP Motes from Disney Mirrorverse.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Motes.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Motes.|

# Seasoning
Aliases: Seasonings
Categories: Currencies
[Seasoning]
Type: [[Currency]]
Seasoning is a [[Currency]] used to progress the [[Harvest Feast|Harvest Feast Milestones]].
## Obtaining
Seasoning is obtained from in-season crops during the [[Harvest Feast]] with a base chance of 0.044% (1 in 2,250 crops broken).
For every Seasoning the player obtains during the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]], they also gain 1 [[Kernels|Kernel]].
### Increasing Drops
Seasoning counts as a [[Rare Crops|Rare Crop]], so all sources of [[Overbloom]] increases the drop chances of Seasoning. Additionally, there are other ways to directly increase the chance to drop Seasoning.
- [[Doug|Natural Talent]] perk: +1%-5% Boost to drop Seasoning
- [[Hashbrown]] [[Reforging|Deep Fried]] reforge: Up to +25% Boost to drop Seasoning
- [[Ted's Contact Baked In Bread]]: +10% Boost to drop Seasoning to one of the four in-season crops
## Usage
Seasoning can be used to progress in [[Harvest Feast|Harvest Feast Milestones]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Added Seasoning.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Seasoning.|

# SkyBlock Gems
Aliases: Gem, Gems, Skyblock Gems
Categories: Currencies
[SkyBlock Gems]
Type: [[Currency]]
SkyBlock Gems are the [[SkyBlock]] premium [[Currency]] and cost real world money to acquire from the Hypixel Store.
## Earning
SkyBlock Gems are available to purchase in bundles of the following quantities from the Hypixel Store. Using a creator code gives a 5% discount off the purchase.
|Quantity|Base Price|Creator Code|
|-|-|-|
|700x|$5.99 USD|$5.69 USD|
|1800x|$14.99 USD|$14.24 USD|
|3750x|$29.99 USD|$28.49 USD|
|7600x|$59.99 USD|$56.99 USD|
|17000x|$119.99 USD|$113.99 USD|
75 SkyBlock Gems are also rewarded to players after talking with [[Elizabeth]] for the first time, this is the only time players get SkyBlock Gems for free.
Players with 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] rank earn 500 SkyBlock Gems per month.
### USD to Coins
Based on the current [[Bazaar]] cost of [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] and the cost of SkyBlock Gems in USD, the value of one USD in SkyBlock [[Coins]] can be calculated.
- 700 Gems ÷ $5.69 = 123.023 Gems per dollar
- () per Cookie from the Bazaar) ÷ (325 Gems per Cookie) = per Gem
- (123.023 Gems per dollar) × ( per Gem) =
- Therefore, one USD is worth .
## Usage
Gems can be spent in different aspects of the game.
### Elizabeth
The player can spend SkyBlock Gems on various actions at [[Elizabeth]]. A [[Booster Cookie]] can be purchased for 325 SkyBlock Gems, and items in [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] can be purchased with Gems. Additionally, [[Account & Profile Upgrades]] can be sped up for at a default cost of 144 SkyBlock Gems per day (10 minutes for 1 SkyBlock Gem), with some upgrades offering cheaper skip costs. Some higher-end Account & Profile Upgrades require Gems to begin.
### Seasonal Bundles
The player can spend SkyBlock Gems on different [[Skin (disambiguation)|Skins]], [[Runes]], and [[Dyes]] at [[Taylor]]'s Seasonal Bundles. These cosmetics are available during network-wide seasonal events like Lunar New Year, Valentine's Day, Spring, Summer, Fall, and Winter.
### Barn Skins
The player can spend SkyBlock Gems on various [[Barn Skins]], which will apply a cosmetic skin that changes how your Barn in [[The Garden]] looks.
## History

|Nov 21, 2025|MVP++ ([[Ranks]]) ranked players now get 500 SkyBlock Gems every month.|
|Apr 8, 2026|Changes to Skyblock Gem bundles resulting in an increase of cost, on a per gem basis, by about 15% across every tier.|

# Sowdust
Aliases: Sawdust
Categories: Currencies
[Sowdust]
Type: [[Currency]]
✿ Sowdust is a [[Currency]] used within [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
✿ Sowdust can be obtained from the following methods:
- Actively harvesting crops on [[The Garden]] using a specialized farming tool
- Harvesting crops in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Mechanics
### Garden
The amount of ✿ Sowdust is calculated per crop block broken. ✿ Sowdust is affected by [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Crop Fortune]].
The formula for calculating ✿ Sowdust gained from farming crops on regular Garden plots using a specialized farming tool is as follows:
GardenSowdustGain=⌈TotalFarmingFortune100⌉BlockBreakMultiplier
TotalFarmingFortune is the player's [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Crop Fortune]] for the matching crop added together.
BlockBreakMultiplier is 2 for [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cactus]], and 1 for all other crops.
### Greenhouse
The amount of ✿ Sowdust gained for harvesting crops and mutations in the Greenhouse depends on the amount of base crops dropped, which is then divided by a crop-specific multiplier and scaled by [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Crop Fortune]].
The formula to calculate the amount for a single crop dropped from a mutation is as follows:
GreenhouseSowdustGain=5×⌊BaseCropAmount5×CropMultiplier⌉×(1+TotalFarmingFortune100)
To calculate the final amount, this needs to be calculated for each crop dropped by a mutation, and then the amounts summed up.
The crop-specific multipliers are as follows:
|Crop|Multiplier|
|-|-|
|[[Wheat]]|1|
|[[Carrot]]|3.5|
|[[Potato]]|3|
|[[Pumpkin]]|0.85|
|[[Sugar Cane]]|2|
|[[Melon Slice]]|4|
|[[Cactus]]|1.5|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|2|
|[[Mushroom]]|0.95|
|[[Nether Wart]]|3|
|[[Moonflower]]|2|
|[[Sunflower]]|2|
|[[Wild Rose]]|2|
### Increasing Sowdust Gain
|Icon|Modifier|Buff|
|-|-|-|
|[[Elephant Pet]]|[[Elephant Pet]]|+0.2-20% ✿ Sowdust|
|[[Large Walnut]]|Earthy Reforge ([[Large Walnut]])|+5% ✿ Sowdust|
|Blooming ([[Weather]])|Blooming Weather ([[Weather]])|+10% ✿ Sowdust|
## Usage
✿ Sowdust can be spent to upgrade [[Garden Chips]] while in [[The Garden]].
|Level|Sowdust Cost|Cumulative Cost|
|-|-|-|
|1|||
|2|100k ✿ Sowdust|100k ✿ Sowdust|
|3|200k ✿ Sowdust|300k ✿ Sowdust|
|4|300k ✿ Sowdust|600k ✿ Sowdust|
|5|400k ✿ Sowdust|1M ✿ Sowdust|
|6|550k ✿ Sowdust|1.55M ✿ Sowdust|
|7|700k ✿ Sowdust|2.25M ✿ Sowdust|
|8|850k ✿ Sowdust|3.1M ✿ Sowdust|
|9|1M ✿ Sowdust|4.1M ✿ Sowdust|
|10|1.15M ✿ Sowdust|5.25M ✿ Sowdust|
|11|1.3M ✿ Sowdust|6.55M ✿ Sowdust|

|15|1.9M ✿ Sowdust|13.25M ✿ Sowdust|
|16|2.05M ✿ Sowdust|15.3M ✿ Sowdust|
|17|2.2M ✿ Sowdust|17.5M ✿ Sowdust|
|18|2.35M ✿ Sowdust|19.85M ✿ Sowdust|

To max all 10 [[Garden Chips]], a total of 250,000,000 ✿ Sowdust is needed.
## Trivia
- The minimum ✿ Sowdust per crop possible is 1 ✿ Sowdust if the player has 100 or less total [[Farming Fortune]].
  - That number is instead 0.5 for [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cactus]].
- With 2600 [[Farming Fortune]] and 20 blocks broken per second, it would take about 133 hours to get the 250,000,000 ✿ Sowdust required to max [[Garden Chips|Chip levels]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]
- Due to the calculations for ✿ Sowdust, the best crop for it is whichever one the player has the most [[Farming Fortune]] in and can get as close to breaking 20 blocks per second.
- It is possible to actually gain a fractional amount of ✿ Sowdust in increments of 0.5 when farming [[Sugar Cane]] or [[Cactus]]. The amount is simply rounded when displayed.

# Souls Rebound
Categories: Disabled Features, Epic items, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Weapons, Craftable items, Bows, Arachnal Items
This item or feature is currently disabled and cannot be used.
[Souls Rebound]
Type: Bow ([[Bows]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Dungeon Level ([[Dungeoneering]]) Requirement: Catacombs VIII (8) ([[Catacombs]]) (when dungeonized)
Stats: [[Damage]]: +450
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] 192x [[Soul String]] 10x [[Dark Queen's Soul]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SOULS_REBOUND
Tooltip: Damage: +450; Your arrows mark enemies you hit for 5 seconds. Marked Enemies don't take damage from you, once the mark expires the target will receive a burst of damage equal to all the damage dealt during the mark increased by up to 10%.; This item is Arachnal !; This item can be reforged!; EPIC BOW
Souls Rebound is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]]. Enemies hit by it are marked and receive any damage dealt to them during the mark after it expires.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|10x [[Dark Queen's Soul]] 192x [[Soul String]] 192x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x64 ×3 + [[Soul String]] x64 ×3 + [[Dark Queen's Soul]] x10 → Souls Rebound|
## Usage
Bow:
Souls Rebound [[Damage]]: +450
[Effects]
[Your arrows mark enemies you hit for 5 seconds. Marked enemies don't take damage from you, once the mark expires the target will receive a burst of damage equal to all the damage dealt during the mark increased by up to 10%.]
The Souls Rebound shoots special arrows which mark enemies they hit for 5 seconds. When a marked enemy is hit by the same player, a ding sound effect plays and the damage is stored. When the 5s is up, the [[Damage]] dealt during the marked time is totaled, increased by 1.1x and dealt in one blow.
[[Dragon Tracer]] cannot be applied to this bow.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Spider Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|20|20|
|✪|10|30|
|✪✪|15|45|
|✪✪✪|20|65|
|✪✪✪✪|25; 10,000 [[Coins]]|90; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|30; 25,000 [[Coins]]|120; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- [[Fire Aspect]], [[Flame]], [[Venomous]] and [[Burning Potion|Burning]] effect will damage the mob even while marked.
- Sleeping Golems in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] will not wake up from any hits while marked except if one of the effects shown above are applied.
- The first arrow shot with this bow will not damage the mob until it's unmarked.
- Using this bow on the [[Sven Packmaster]] and dealing enough damage used to be able to skip the second phase, allowing the player to kill the boss faster.
  - This has been changed since, and will display a message in chat reading "This mob is immune to Souls Rebound!"
- This bow shares a similar name with one of Yone's abilities, Soul Unbound, from League of Legends. The ability marks enemies and "stores" damage based on damage dealt during the mark, dealing a burst of damage after the mark expires, which is similar to the Souls Rebound's ability.
- The [[Souls Rebound]] Souls Rebound has been Disabled since July 25th, 2024 or 824 days as of today, after being Enabled for 1,222 days. Following this trend, on November 30th, 2027 onward, it will have been Disabled for longer than it has been Enabled for.
- Souls rebound has the longest time of any weapon for being Disabled.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Souls Rebound as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Souls Rebound.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Mob invincibility frames are now correctly calculated during marked time.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Souls Rebound can now be dungeonized.|
|Jul 25, 2024|Disabled Souls Rebound.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Souls Rebound as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Bucket (disambiguation)
Aliases: Bucket (Disambiguation), Magical Bucket (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Bucket may refer to the following:
- [[Bucket]] - an item used to pick up/obtain and move liquids.
- [[Water Bucket]] - an item used to store, transport, pick up and place [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]].
- [[Lava Bucket]] - an item used to store, transport, pick up and place [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]].
- [[Milk Bucket]] - an item obtained from milking a [[Cow]] that clears all active effects.
- [[Magical Water Bucket]] - an item used to place [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]], but has infinite capacity.
- [[Magical Lava Bucket]] - an item used to place [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|lava]], but has infinite capacity.
- [[Magical Milk Bucket]] - an infinite Milk Bucket obtained from [[Shania]].
- [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] - an infinite [[Minion Fuel]] that increases minion speed by 25%
- [[Magma Bucket]] - an infinite [[Minion Fuel]] that increases minion speed by 30%
- [[Plasma Bucket]] - an infinite [[Minion Fuel]] that increases minion speed by 35%
- [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]] - Placed near fishing spot to obtain [[Chum]] from killing [[Sea Creatures]].
- [[Chum Bucket|Full Chum Bucket]] - A full Chum Bucket after filled with 10 [[Chum]].
- [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]] - Works like [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]] but also gives a chance to fish up [[Agarimoo]].
- [[Test Bucket Please Ignore]] - an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] accessory given from the [[Mirrorverse]].
- [[Plumber's Bucket]] - An item bought from [[Plumber Joe (Rift)|Plumber Joe]] in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
- [[Frogspawn Bucket]] - An item used to upgrade [[Frog Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
- [[Portable Washer]] - An item bought from [[The Garden|Skymart]] to rid of all active [[Sprayonators|Sprayonator]] effects in [[The Garden]].
- Attributes:
  - [[Coralot Shard]] ([[Attributes|Bucket Lover]])
  - [[Sepialot Shard]] ([[Attributes|Bucket Greed]])
  - [[Goldolot Shard]] ([[Attributes|Echo of Bucket]])

# Candy (disambiguation)

Candy refers to many items in the game. During the [[Spooky Festival]], players can find [[Green Candy]] and [[Purple Candy]] by defeating enemies, to gain score towards the Spooky Festival. They can also be used as a currency at the [[Fear Mongerer]]. For more information about Green and Purple Candy, see [[Spooky Festival|Spooky Festival#Candy]]. Candy can also refer to:
- Other [[Spooky Festival]] items:
  - [[Candy the Fish]] - A Collectable from [[The Fish Family]] obtained from the [[Spooky Festival]].
  - [[Candy Corn]] - A [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] obtained from the [[Spooky Festival]].
  - [[Rock Candy]] - A [[Power Stones|Power Stone]] obtained from the [[Spooky Festival]].
- Carrot Candy - Items released in the V0.7.8 update, in the [[Carrot]] collection.
  - [[Simple Carrot Candy]]
  - [[Great Carrot Candy]]
  - [[Superb Carrot Candy]]
  - [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]
  - [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]] - an item used to upgrade [[Superb Carrot Candy]] to [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]].
- [[Accessories]]:
  - [[Candy Talisman]]
  - [[Candy Ring]]
  - [[Candy Artifact]]
  - [[Candy Relic]]
- Other:
  - [[Jerry Candy]] - A [[Consumables|consumable]] item obtained from [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]].
  - [[Poisoned Candy]] - A craftable item used at [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]] event.
  - [[Dark Candy]] - Earned during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]] and given to [[Vargul the Unearthed]] for rewards.
  - [[Candycomb]] - A [[Salts|Salt]] used for [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].

# Divan (disambiguation)
Aliases: Divan, Divans
Categories: Disambiguations, Armor
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Divan was a legendary dwarf and former king. He was the first to enter the [[Crystal Hollows]] and discover [[Divan Fragment]] and [[Divan's Alloy]]. Additionally, he is [[Dulin]]'s ancestor.
It may also refer to:
- [[Armor of Divan]], the best [[Mining]] set in the game, obtained through [[The Forge]].
  - [[Divan Fragment]], an item obtained from [[Crystal Nucleus]], used to forge the Divan Armor.
- [[Divan's Drill]], the best [[Mining]] tool in the game, obtained through [[The Forge]].
  - [[Divan's Alloy]], an item obtained from [[Crystal Nucleus]], used to forge the Divan's Drill.
- [[Pendant of Divan]], the best [[Mining]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]] in the game, obtained through [[The Forge]].
- [[Divan's Powder Coating]], an item used to grant +500 [[Mining Speed]] to [[Drills]] and +10 [[Mining Fortune]] to Divan Armor pieces.
- [[Mines of Divan]], an area in [[Crystal Hollows]] and the source of [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]].
  - [[Keepers of Divan]], are the NPCs that spawn the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]] once given all 4 [[Scavenged Items]].
- [[Divan's Gateway]], a small area in [[Dwarven Mines]], located south of the forge and directly above [[The Mist]].
Linked from: [[Dead Man's Hunt]], [[Dulin]]

# Dragon Pets (disambiguation)
Aliases: Dragon Pet, Dragon Pets, Dragon pet, Symbiosis
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Dragon Pets are a type of [[Pets|Pet]] that start as an Egg. While as an Egg, Dragon Pets have no stats nor perks and cannot hold [[Pet Items]]. Upon reaching level 100, a Dragon Pet will hatch, granting it stats and perks. Dragon Pets can be leveled up to level 200, with the exception of the [[Ender Dragon Pet]]. After level 101, every level will require the same amount of Pet XP. Every Dragon Pet also gains at level 200 the Ability: Symbiosis, giving additional effect depending on how many pets of the same skill type the player owns at max level.
- [[Jade Dragon Pet]] - A [[Foraging]] pet mostly focused on [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[Sweep]].
- [[Golden Dragon Pet]] - A [[Combat]] pet mostly focused on [[Magic Find]], [[Strength]], [[Attack Speed]] and [[Damage]].
- [[Rose Dragon Pet]] - A [[Farming]] pet mostly focused on [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Overbloom]].
Dragon Pets could also mean:
- [[Ender Dragon Pet]] - A [[Combat]] pet that drops from the [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]]. It is the only Dragon Pet that goes to level 100.

# Eye (disambiguation)

Eye may refer to:

- [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] - upgraded variant of [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]].
- [[Summoning Eye]] - an item used to summon [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]] in [[Dragon's Nest]].
  - [[Sleeping Eye]] - replaces a placed [[Summoning Eye]] in the player's inventory until an [[Ender Dragon]] is summoned and turns into a [[Remnant of the Eye]].
- [[Remnant of the Eye]] - an item that keeps the player alive right before death, only works in [[The End]] island.
- [[Precursor Eye]] - a [[Helmets|Helmet]] obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]], it has an ability to shoot laser when sneaking.
- [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] - a material used to turn a [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] into a [[Hyperion]].
- [[Spider Eye]] - a common drop form [[Spider|Spiders]] and [[Cave Spider|Cave Spiders]].

- [[Capsaicin Eyedrops]] - an item used to boost the speed of [[Inferno Minion]] Inferno Minion.
- [[Four-Eyed Fish]] - a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] for [[Griffin Pet]].
- [[Beady Eyes]] - a [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that gives the Beady reforge to [[Vacuums]].
- [[Awakened Summoning Eye]] - an unreleased item used to summon multiple [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]] and [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragon]].
- [[Giant's Eye Sword]] - an unreleased [[Swords|Sword]].

# Gems (disambiguation)

Gems may refer to:
- [[SkyBlock Gems]] - Premium currency used in [[Community Center]].
- [[Reaper Gem]] - A [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that gives player 8 [[Ferocity]] for 5 seconds after getting a kill.
- [[Pure Mithril Gem]] - An item used to upgrade [[Mithril Golem Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
- [[Mangrove Gem]] - A [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that gives the Groovy Reforge to [[Armor]].
- [[Runes|Gem Rune]] - A level 4 rune, can be applied to [[Bows]].
- [[Purple Gem]] - Used to open the door in the Cavern Biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
- [[Orange Gem]] - Used to open the door in the Cavern Biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
- [[Lime Gem]] - Used to open the door in the Cavern Biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
Gemstones may refer to:
- [[Gemstone]] - Resource used in crafting mined in [[Crystal Hollows]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]].
- [[Gemstone Fortune]] - A [[Stats|Stat]] that grants [[Mining Fortune]] to Gemstones.
- [[Gemstone Spread]] - A [[Stats|Stat]] that grants [[Mining Spread]] to Gemstones exclusively.
- [[Gemstone Mixture]] - An item [[The Forge|forged]] and used in various recipes.
- [[Gemstone Crystals|Gemstone Crystal]] - Resource used in unlocking [[Crystal Nucleus]] reward bundles and forging Perfect [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
- [[Gemstone Slot]] - A game mechanic where certain [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] can be placed in an item to boost the stats based on the Gemstone.
- [[Gemstone Drill LT-522]] - A drill [[The Forge|forged]] and gives extra ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]], [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]] when mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
- [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] - An item that works as both a [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] and a [[Swords|Sword]], unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone IX (9)]].
- [[Gemstone Chamber]] - An item [[The Forge|forged]] and used to unlock [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]] in [[Armor of Divan]].
- [[Gemstone Equipment]] - A mining [[Equipment]] set.
- ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] - A collectable used to upgrade tier 6 and 7 perks in [[Heart of the Mountain]].
- [[Gemstone Fuel Tank]] - A fuel tank used in [[Drills]] to increases their [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] capacity to 50,000.
- [[Gemstone Sack|Large Gemstone Sack]] - A sack that holds all types of [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] except Flawless and Perfect Gemstones.
- [[Glossy Gemstone]] - A material obtained during [[Mining Fiesta]] while mining Gemstones.
- [[Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams]] - A consumable that grants permanent [[Gemstone Fortune]].
- [[Gemstone Divan Helmet Skin]] - An [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] for [[Armor of Divan|Helmet of Divan]].

# Hat (disambiguation)
Aliases: Hat
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Hat can refer to many things in SkyBlock, but most commonly a mainly-cosmetic [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked early in a [[Collections|Collection]].
## Helmets
- [[Blaze Hat]]
- [[Blobfish Hat]]
- [[Chicken Hat]]: Drops an [[Egg]] when the player sneaks and reduces fall damage by 5%.
- [[Cow Hat]]: Grants immunity to de-buffs.
- [[Creeper Hat]]: Grants immunity to explosion damage.
- [[Fish Hat]]: Has a Splash ability, which is used in the [[Melancholic Viking]]'s quest.
- [[Golem Hat]]: Drops an [[Iron Ingot]] when the player sneaks.
- [[Leaflet Armor|Leaflet Hat]]: Part of the [[Leaflet Armor]] set.
- [[Magmafish Hat]]: Has a Splash ability.
- [[Mole Hat]]: Part of the [[Park Armor]] set.
- [[Party Top Hat]]: Part of the [[Party Equipment]] set.
- [[Pufferfish Hat]]: Has a Spiky ability, which gives a small chance to deal an additional +10 [[Damage]], plus 20% [[Strength]] to nearby enemies.
- [[Rabbit Hat]]: Grants Jump Boost IV.
- [[Salmon Hat]]
- [[Sea Lantern Hat]]: Allows the player to breathe underwater for 5x as long.
- [[Skeleton Hat]]: Grants a 20% chance for arrows to explode on impact, dealing 50 [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]] to enemies within 8 blocks.
- [[Slime Hat]]: Grants knockback immunity.
- [[Spider Pet]]: Grants a -30% damage reduction to [[Arthropod]] mobs.
- [[Squid Hat]]: Grants an extra chance to fish up [[Squid|Squids]].
- [[Tropical Fish Hat]]
- [[Seymour's Special Armor|Velvet Top Hat]]: Part of the [[Seymour's Special Armor]] set.
- [[Zombie Hat]]: Grants +10 [[Defense]] for every [[Zombie]] within 8 blocks.
## [[Hatcessories]]
- [[Crab Hat of Celebration]]
- [[Crab Hat of Celebration|Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition]]
- [[Sloth Hat of Celebration]]
- [[5th Anniversary Balloon Hat]]
- [[6th Anniversary Balloon Hat]]
- [[7th Anniversary Cake Hat]]
## Other
- [[Magic Top Hat]]: Used to upgrade the [[Snowman Pet]] from [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] at [[Kat]].
- [[Hattie]]: An [[NPC]] in the [[Backwater Bayou]] that teaches about [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]].
- [[Furniture|Witch Hat]]: A [[Furniture]].

# Helios (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Helios is referred to, through the [[Harvest Feast]] and items such as the [[Sun's Grasp]], as a legendary farmer who was capable of achieving exceptional results from farming using his bare hands, and would bake [[Bread Bowl|Bread Bowls]] to close friends.
Helios may refer to:
- [[Ray of Helios]], a rare item obtained from harvesting in-season crops, with a 1/500,000,000 chance of dropping.
  - [[Sun's Grasp]], the [[Gloves]] crafted with a [[Ray of Helios]].
- [[Bread Bowl]], an item purchased for 10,000 [[Kernels]], existing purely to gift to other people, with both the donor and recipient receiving nothing apart from a message in chat.
It is unknown whether the [[Spaceman]] is connected to Helios.

# Jungle (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Jungle could refer to one of two locations:
- [[Jungle Island]] A sub location of The Park, where Jungle Wood can be collected, featuring [[Romero]] and [[Juliette]].
  - [[Jungle Log]] A variant of wood, with its own collection.
    - [[Enchanted Jungle Log]] Enchanted variant.
  - [[Leaves|Jungle Leaves]] A variant of leaves used to get [[Vines]] on the [[Trades]] menu.
  - [[Jungle Minion]] A minion for said collection.
  - [[Spruce Axe|Jungle Axe]] older name for [[Spruce Axe]] — a useful tool for Foraging from Spruce Wood Collection.
  - [[Travel Scroll to Jungle Island]] An item that unlocks [[Fast Travel]] to the Jungle Island.
  - [[Portals|Portal to Jungle Island]] A portal that can be placed on the [[Private Island]] to warp to said location.
  - [[Biome Stick|Jungle Biome Stick]] An item used to convert areas of the Private Island into Jungle Biome.
- [[Jungle]] A part of [[Crystal Hollows]] featuring [[Gemstone|Amethyst]], [[Sludge]] and more.
  - [[Jungle Temple]] A sub location containing parkour that leads to the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]].
  - [[Key Guardian]] A mob that rewards Jungle Keys when killed.
  - [[Jungle Key]] An item used to get access into the Jungle Temple.
  - [[Jungle Pickaxe]] A tool that may drop [[Sludge Juice]] when used to mine in the Jungle.
  - [[Jungle Heart]] An item used to buy Jungle Amulet and [[Tribal Spear]].
  - [[Jungle Amulet]] An accessory that grants +10 [[Mining Fortune]] when mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
Linked from: [[Wood (disambiguation)]]

- [[Kuudra]] - A Boss that spawns in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
- [[Kuudra Keys]] - Used to open [[Kuudra|Kuudra Reward Chests]].
- [[Kuudra Pet]] - A Pet obtained and upgraded from the Kuudra Boss [[Collections|Collection]].
- [[Kuudra Chunk]] - Used to upgrade the [[Kuudra Pet]] to higher rarities.
- [[Crag|Kuudra Gatekeeper]] - An NPC that sends parties to fight [[Kuudra]].
- [[Kuudra Believer]] - An NPC that rewards [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Helmet]] after certain conditions are met.
- [[Kuudra Archeologist]] - An NPC that rewards [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Leggings]] after certain conditions are met.
- [[Kuudra Loremaster]] - An NPC that rewards [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Boots]] after certain conditions are met.
- [[Kuudra Mandible]] - A [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that gives the Chomp reforge to [[Fishing Rods]].
- [[Burning Kuudra Core]] - An [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants [[Hunting Fortune]].
  - [[Fiery Kuudra Core]]
  - [[Infernal Kuudra Core]]

  - [[Kuudra's Heart]]

- [[SkyBlock Levels|Ӄ Master Kuudra Slayer Emblem]], an emblem obtained after reaching 5000 [[Collections|Boss Collection]] progress.
Kuudra Teeth may refer to:
- [[Kuudra Teeth]] - Dropped from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Reward Chests]].
- [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] - A series of items required to obtained [[Kuudra Follower Relic]].
Kuudra Follower may refer to:
- [[Kuudra Follower]] - A [[Mob]] that spawns in the Kuudra boss fight.
- [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] - An [[Armor]] obtained around [[Crimson Isle]] for certain conditions.
- [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] - An [[Accessories|Accessory]] that halves the damage received from [[Kuudra Follower|Kuudra Followers]].
- [[Kuudra Follower Relic]] - Upgraded Accessory of the Artifact, reduces damage received from [[Kuudra Follower|Kuudra Followers]] by 65%.

- [[Omegagma]] - a [[Mob]] in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
- [[Kuudra Slasher]] - a [[Mob]] in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].

# Potions (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
A [[Potions|Potion]] is a drink used to gain buffs/nerfs for a set period of time, they could mean:
- [[God Potion]] - Gives the player a large amount of buffs from various potion effects.
  - [[Mixins]] - Gives additional buffs that require [[God Potion]] to be active.
- [[Brews]] - Gives additional buffs to potions.
- Healing Potion:
  - [[Healing Potion]] - Instantly heals players when consumed or thrown.
  - [[Ghosts|Healing Potion (Healer)]] - A [[Ghosts|Ghost Ability]] for [[Healer|Healers]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- Strength Potion:
  - [[Strength Potion]] - Increases players' [[Strength]].
  - [[Ghosts|Strength Potion (Berserk)]] - A [[Ghosts|Ghost Ability]] for [[Berserk|berserks]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- Stun Potion:
  - [[Stun Potion]] - Gives a chance for players to stun enemies when hit, or stuns enemies when thrown.
  - [[Ghosts|Stun Potion (Tank)]] - A [[Ghosts|Ghost Ability]] for [[Tank|Tanks]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- Absorption Potion:
  - [[Absorption Potion]] - Gives players [[Absorption]].
  - [[Ghosts|Absorption Potion (Tank)]] - A [[Ghosts|Ghost Ability]] for [[Tank|Tanks]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- [[Recall Potion]] - A consumable that teleport players to a saved location.
- [[Anti-Morph Potion]] - A potion used to obtain [[Used Detransfigured Face]] in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
- [[Secret I Potion]] - An undrinkable potion used to obtain [[Alchemist Recipe]] from [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].
- [[Great Spook Potion]] - Gives players +3 [[Fear]] for 24 hours.
It could also mean:
- [[Potion Affinity Talisman]]
  - [[Potion Affinity Ring]]
  - [[Potion Affinity Artifact]]
- [[Potion Bag]]

# Precursor (disambiguation)
Aliases: Precursor
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Precursors were a mysterious species, mentioned in dialogues of various NPCs such as [[Julian]], [[Sadan]], [[Dr. Emmett]], [[Oruo The Almighty]], or [[Loras]]. However, they cannot be encountered in game. The term might also refer to:
### Items
- [[Precursor Apparatus]] - A material that combines all [[Automaton|robot parts]] into one.
- [[Precursor Eye]] - A [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] with an ability to shoot laser to deal damage.
- [[Precursor Gear]] - A [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that grants the [[Reforging|Ancient]] reforge to [[Armor]].
- [[Precursor Relic]] - Items that are used to upgrade [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] and [[Wither Armor]] into one of four variants.
- [[Precursor Drone Pet]] - A [[Pets|Pet]] added by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] thirtyvirus during [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]'s mayorship.
### Locations
- [[Precursor Remnants]] - A location in [[Crystal Hollows]] that generate [[Gemstone|Sapphire]] and spawns [[Automaton|Automatons]].
- [[Lost Precursor City]] - A sub-location in [[Precursor Remnants]] that contains the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
- Precursor Ruins - One of the race location for the [[Dungeon Hub Race]].
Linked from: [[Galatea]], [[Loras]], [[Moonglade Marsh]], [[NPC/List/Moonglade Marsh]], [[Tangleburg]], [[Tangleburg Library]]

# Primal (disambiguation)
Aliases: Primal
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Primal can refer to a variety of topics:
## Primal Fears
- [[Primal Fear]] - A mob that can spawn during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]] event based on the [[Fear]] stat. Manifests into different forms on death:
  - [[Math Teacher]]
  - [[Darkness Shade]]
  - [[Public Speaking Demon]]
  - [[Deadline]]
  - [[Commitment Phobia]]
  - [[Vegan Crawler]]
- [[Eerie Pet]] - A pet that can increase the damage dealt to Primal Fears.
- [[Dark Candy]] - Dropped from Primal Fears.
## Primal Dragons
The update involving Primal Dragons and their related features was shelved due to performance issues and never made it past [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] testing.
- [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragon]] - A type of dragon spawned by [[Awakened Summoning Eye|Awakened Summoning Eyes]].
- [[Primal Dragon Egg]] - An item dropped from Primal Dragons that would upgrade the [[Ender Dragon Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
- [[Primal Dragon Armor]]
  - [[Dragon Fragment|Primal Dragon Fragment]] - Used to craft Primal Dragon Armor.
- [[Blade of Dragonfire]] - An upgrade to [[Aspect of the Dragons]].
  - [[Primal Dragon Heart]] - Used to craft Blade of Dragonfire.
## Other
- Primal Force ( [[Lion Pet]] perk)
- [[Runes|Primal Fear Rune III]]

# Professor (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Professor is a title worn by many NPCs. Within [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], [[Sadan]] is said to have a professor that he beat in a duel and [[Necron]] has both professors with whom he had repeated heated arguments and teachers. In [[Mort]]'s menu, [[Scarf]] is said to have a teacher. A professor, presumably [[The Professor]], is also mentioned in the tooltip of [[Scarf's Grimoire]]. Some professors are likely associated with the Mage Academy. Professor can refer to:
- [[The Professor]], the boss of [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]. Few items and NPCs bear the title of a Professor:
  - [[Golden Heads|Golden Professor Head]]
  - [[Diamond Heads|Diamond Professor Head]]
- [[Professor Robot]], an NPC in the [[Lost Precursor City]] that allows the player to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
- [[Professor Wynd]], an NPC who introduces the player to [[Weather]].

# Races (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations, Race
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
[[Island Racing|Races]] are timed running trials. There are four races that take place at numerous places around skyblock and three ones with 8 unique challenges in the [[Dungeon Hub]].
- [[End Race]] - Race that takes place in [[The End]]. Talk to [[Guber]].
- [[Woods Race|Woods Race‎]] - Race that takes place in [[Spruce Woods]]. Talk to [[Gustave]].
- [[Chicken Race|Chicken Race‎]] - Race that takes place in [[Jerry's Workshop]]. Talk to [[Gulliver]].
- [[Dungeon Hub Race]] - Race that takes place in [[Dungeon Hub]]. Talk to [[Guildford]].
- [[Rift Race]] - Race that takes place in [[West Village]]. Talk to [[Gunther]].
It may also refer to:
- [[Racing Helmet]] - a [[Helmets|Helmet]] obtained from [[Shen's Auction]].

    - [[Revenant Horror|Atoned Horror]] - [[Slayer]] [[Bosses|BOSS]] that appears when doing [[Zombie Slayer]] Tier V.
  - [[Revenant Sycophant]] - [[Zombie Slayer]] Tier III [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].
  - [[Revenant Champion]] - [[Zombie Slayer]] Tier IV [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].
  - [[Deformed Revenant]] - [[Zombie Slayer]] Tier IV [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].
  - [[Atoned Revenant]] - [[Zombie Slayer]] Tier V [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].
  - [[Atoned Champion]] - [[Zombie Slayer]] Tier V [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].
- [[Revenant Flesh]] - Common drop from the [[Revenant Horror]], is used to craft various items.
  - [[Revenant Viscera]] - Upgraded form of [[Revenant Flesh]], can be used to craft better gear.
- [[Revenant Falchion]] - A [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer III (3)]].
  - [[Reaper Falchion]] - An [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VI (6)]] and upgraded version of [[Revenant Falchion]].
- [[Revenant Armor]] - [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IV (4)]] ([[Leggings]],[[Boots]]) and [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer V (5)]] ([[Chestplates|Chestplate]]).
  - [[Reaper Armor]] - [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VII (7)]] and upgraded version of [[Revenant Armor]].
- [[Revenant Catalyst]] - Crafting material.
- [[Revenant Minion]] - A combat [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns Revenants.

# Shiny (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Shiny may refer to:
### [[Year of the Pig]]
- [[Shiny Pig]], a [[Mob]] spawning during the Year of the Pig event.
- [[Shiny Orb]], a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item used on [[Shiny Pig]] to obtain rewards.
- [[Shiny Token]], a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to buy items from [[Piggy]].
- [[Shiny Rod]], a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item helping to catch [[Shiny Pig]] and increasing [[Coins]] from [[Shiny Orb]].
### [[Reforging|Reforge]]
- [[Reforging|Shiny]], a reforge that used to be applied to [[Accessories]] from the [[Blacksmith]].
- [[Shiny Prism]], a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] giving the [[Shiny Prism|Glistening]] reforge to [[Equipment]] pieces.
### [[Attributes|Shard]]
- [[Shinyfish Shard]], a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] increasing [[Attributes|Help From Above]] attribute when syphoned.
### Materials
- [[Shiniest]], a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Material needed to claim the [[Jade Dragon Pet]] from the [[Jade Dragon]].
### Other
- [[Shiny Relic]], a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Mementos|Memento]] obtained from [[Shiny Pig]] during the "Post-uprising" period.
- [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]], a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item used in the [[Ring of Love|Legendary Ring of Love]] questline.
- [[Shining Coin]], a quest item in the [[Critter Safari]], used to spawn a [[Gimmiegold]].
- [[Achievements|Oh Shiny!]] Achievement, awarded from mining an [[Ender Nodes|Ender Node]] in [[The End]].
- "Shiny" prefix, a variant of [[Necron's Handle]] and [[Wither Armor]] that has a 5% chance to appear on [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Master Mode Floor 7]] chests.
  - The prefix is kept on crafted items, including: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]], [[Astraea]], [[Hyperion]], [[Scylla]], [[Valkyrie]], [[Goldor's Armor]], [[Maxor's Armor]], [[Necron's Armor]], [[Storm's Armor]].
### Dialogue
- [[Piggy]]: [[Shiny Orb]]
- [[Shiny Pig]]: [[Shiny Orb]]
- [[Blood God Shrine]]: [[Shiny Orb]], [[Shiny Pig]] and [[Shiny Relic]]
- [[Juliette]]: [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]]
- [[Chipper McPickaxe]]
- [[Lukas the Aquarist]]
- [[Jerry]] (on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]])
- [[Fetchur]] ( [[Flint and Steel]] request)
### Direct mention of the word "Shiny"

- [[Sprays|Golden Egg Spray]]

Mobs:

- [[Spirit Bat]] - a mob that spawns during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- [[Spirit Bull]] - a mob that spawns during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- [[Spirit Chicken]] - a mob that spawns during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- [[Spirit Rabbit]] - a mob that spawns during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- [[Spirit Sheep]] - a mob that spawns during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- [[Spirit Wolf]]- a mob that spawns during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- [[Pack Spirit]] - a mob that spawns in [[Howling Cave]].
- [[Howling Spirit]] - a mob that spawns [[Howling Cave]].
- [[Phantom Spirit]] - a mob that spawns during [[Spooky Festival]] and copies the player's skin, armor and held item.

Items:

- [[Spirit Bone]] - a material found in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] used in various recipes.
- [[Spirit Wing]] - a material found in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] used in various recipes.
- [[Spirit Sword]] - a dungeon [[Swords|Sword]] that lets you get an additional ability as a ghost in [[Dungeons]].
- [[Spirit Sceptre]] - a dungeon [[Swords|Sword]] that shoots guided [[Spirit Bat|Spirit Bats]] to explode at enemies.
- [[Spirit Bow]] - a weapon used to deal damage to [[Thorn]].
- [[Spirit Shortbow]] - a dungeon [[Shortbows|Shortbow]] that lets you get an additional ability as a ghost in [[Dungeons]].
- [[Spirit Mask]] - a [[Helmets|Helmet]] obtained from the [[Dark Auction]].
- [[Spirit Boots]] - a dungeon [[Boots]] that lets the player fly for 5 seconds, even as a ghost in [[Dungeons]].
- [[Spirit Stone]] - a [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that gives the Spiritual reforge.
- [[Spirit Pet]] - a [[Pets|Pet]] that activates when player turns into a ghost in [[Dungeons]], the pet doesn't need to be equipped for the perks to work.
- [[Spirit Potion]] - a [[Potions|Potion]] that buffs [[Speed]] and [[Crit Damage]].
- [[Runes|Spirit Rune]] - a level 12 [[Runes|Rune]] obtained from [[Sven Packmaster]], can be applied to [[Weapons]].

# Transmission (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations, Abilities
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
Transmission are abilities that let the player teleport a certain amount of blocks:
- Instant Transmission - Used in [[Aspect of the End]] and [[Aspect of the Void]] to teleport up to 8 blocks forward.
- Ether Transmission - Used in [[Etherwarp Conduit]] to teleport to a block up to 57 blocks away, can be merged into [[Aspect of the End]] and [[Aspect of the Void]].
- Sinrecall Transmission - Used in [[Sinseeker Scythe]] to teleport up to 4 blocks forward temporarily to deal melee damage.
- Weird Transmission - Used in [[Aspect of the Leech]] to teleport up to 3 blocks forward in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
- [[Transmission Tuner]] - Can be applied to items with Transmission ability to increase the distance they can teleport by up to 4 extra blocks, or 1 extra block in the case of [[Aspect of the Leech]].
Linked from: [[End Stone Geode]]

The term Treasure may refer to:
- [[Treasure]]: A type of [[Fishing]] loot.
  - [[Treasure Bait]]: A [[Bait|Fishing Bait]] that grants +10 [[Fishing Speed]] and +2 [[Treasure Chance]].
  - [[Treasure Hook]]: A [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] that only allows the player to fish items and treasure.
- [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]]: Various items found in [[Ice]] in the [[Glacial Cave]].
- [[Treasure Chest]]: A mechanic in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
- [[Treasure Hunter]]: An NPC that gives you a hint to where a treasure in [[Mushroom Desert]] can be found.
- [[Treasure Hoarder]]: A Mob in the [[Upper Mines]] of [[Dwarven Mines]].
- [[Treasurite]]: A resource dropped from [[Treasure Hoarder|Treasure Hoarders]].
- [[Team Treasurite]]: Various Mobs that spawn around [[Mithril Deposits]], including [[Boss Corleone|Corleone]].
- [[Dwarven Treasure]]: A [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] for [[Equipment]].
- [[Treasure Talisman]]: A Talisman that can be rarely found in chests in [[Dungeons]].

# Wizard (disambiguation)
Categories: Disambiguations
- This article is a disambiguation page; a list of articles with the same or similar name.; If an internal link led you here, you may wish to change the link to point to the intended article.
The term Wizard may refer to:
- [[Wizard]] - an NPC that lets you travel to [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
- [[Wizard (Catacombs)]] - an NPC tht grants [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing of Wisdom I]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
  - [[Wizard's Crystal]] - an Item found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] that can be given to the [[Wizard (Catacombs)]].
- [[Wizard's Assistant]] - an NPC that replaces [[Wizard]] after closing [[The Breach]].
- [[Wizard But Weird]] - an NPC in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] that shows the player the [[Rift Guide]].
- [[Wizardman]] - an NPC in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] that plays a major role once the player reaches [[The Mountaintop]].
- [[Lost Wizard]] - an NPC in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] in the [[Walk of Fame]], requiring a [[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]] to proceed.
- [[Wizard Tower]] - a location in [[Hub Island|The Hub]] that contains the portal to [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
  - [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] - [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]]'s equivalent of the [[Wizard Tower]], containing a [[Teleport Pad]] to the [[Rift Gallery Entrance]].
- [[Wizardman Bureau]] - a location in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] where [[Wizardman]] awaits for his trials.
- [[Wizard's Breadcrumbs]] - an item used in the beginning of [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
- [[Wizard Face]] - a helmet obtained after gaining all other pieces of [[Wizardman]]'s Armor.
  - [[Warm Wizard Face]] - a helmet that combines [[Wizard Face]] and [[Anti-Bite Scarf]]'s abilities.
    - [[Warmer Wizard Face]] - a helmet that combines [[Wizard Face]] and [[Anti-Bite Scarf|Anti-Bite Scarf (Mythic)]]'s abilities.
- [[Wizardman Leggings]] - one of the armor pieces gained from [[Wizardman]]'s trials.
- [[Wizard Wand]] - an item that can be consumed, making it disappear for 3-5 days, and returns with an additional +1 [[Intelligence]] (up to 100 [[Intelligence|Int]]).
- [[Piece of Wizard Portal]] - a [[Mementos|Memento]] given to players who contributed to the [[City Projects|Repair Wizard Portal City Project]].

# Admin
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Admin]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 89-220
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 45,000-800,000 (Base); [[Defense]]: 150; [[Damage]]: 936-11,700
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Undead Essence]] 0–1x [[Revive Stone]] 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]]
XP: 133-330 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 97-242
[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_UNDEAD_<NAME>_25; MASTER_CRYPT_UNDEAD_<NAME>_25
Admins are [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] that can be found in a specific room in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They are based on some current and previous Hypixel staff members with 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] rank (now 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]]).
## Spawning
Admins can only be found in the Admin Room or Builder room in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They all wield a bone like their other counterparts.
There are 34 admins in the Admin Room, including Linfoot, Dctr, Jayavarmen, Minikloon, aPunch, Likaos, Nitroholic, sfarnham, Relenter, hypixel, Codename_B, LadyBleu, ChiLynn, Cecer, Sylent, LuckyKessie, Thorlon, OrangeMarshall, Externalizable, Jamiethegeek, Plancke, Williamtiger, AgentK, RobityBobity, Bembo, Revengeee, Rezzus, Judg3, Erosemberg, Bloozing, Skyerzz, Donpireso, Pjoke1, and Flameboy101.
The Builder Room contains 10 admins, including AdamWho, BlocksKey, Cheesey, DEADORKAI, DistrictGecko, falloutowns, fudgiethewhale, MistressEldrid, Plummel, and Vinny.
## Behavior
Like [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]], Admins attack by running towards the player. Although they wield a [[Bone]], they won't attack the player with it. They can also attack the player with a bow if they are far from it.
## Stats
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Defense]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv89]|The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]), [[Mithril Deposits]]|45,000|150|936|133|97|29|CRYPT_UNDEAD_<NAME>_25|
|[Lv220]|The Master Mode Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])|800,000|150|11,700|330|242|73|MASTER_CRYPT_UNDEAD_<NAME>_25|
Similar to [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]], however, Admins have a [[Health]] multiplier based on the floor they spawn in, using the following formula, rounding down:
Health=⌊BaseHealth×1.5(Floor−1)⌋
For [[The Catacombs - Entrance]], the [[Health]] is simply half of the base value, so 22,500 .
This results in the following [[Health]] values for each [[Catacombs|Floor]]:

Admins drop 1,000 [[Experience]] (except for hypixel, who drops 10,000 [[Experience]]), and 1 [[Coins|Coin]]. They give +40 [[Combat|Combat XP]] in Normal Mode and +60 [[Combat|Combat XP]] in Master Mode. They have the same item drops as the normal Crypt Undead.
## Trivia
- aPunch doesn't have a red name like the rest of the admins, instead, he has a bold pink name. However, he is not considered a [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].

# Angry Archaeologist
Aliases: Angry Archaologist, Angry Archeologist, Diamond Guy
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Minibosses
[Angry Archaeologist]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 63-673
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 8,500-18,000,000; [[Defense]]: 900-1,200; [[Damage]]: 201-165,600
[Drops]
Drops: 3–5x [[Diamond Essence]] 0–1x [[Diamond Atom]] 0–1x [[Beating Heart]]
XP: 472-5,047 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 189-2019
[Metadata]
ID: DIAMOND_GUY_#; MASTER_DIAMOND_GUY_#
The Angry Archaeologist is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Spawning
The Angry Archaeologist can spawn in normal and boss rooms within any floor of The Catacombs.
## Behavior
The Angry Archaeologist will run at the player and attack them. It appears to use a [[Diamond Sword]] and random tiered [[Perfect Armor]]. When at low health, it uses 3 Splash [[Healing Potion|Healing Potions]] quickly. Players can leech off of its [[Healing]] when it does this.

## Trivia
- This mob is most likely a reference to the Crazy Archaeologist, a mini-boss from RuneScape who reside in the ruins of a forgotten cemetery.
- In periods of lag, you can see the total amount of enemies in the room you are standing in via tablist. The Angry Archaeologist's name in tablist is "Diamond Guy". Using an API viewer site such as sky.lea.moe or sky.shiiyu.moe, Angry Archaeologist kills are displayed "Diamond Guy" kills.
- Angry Archaeologist seems to be using diamond pickaxe instead of diamond sword in the [[The Catacombs - Entrance]].

# Bigfoot
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Bigfoot]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])

Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])

[Drops]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 1629

ID: SADAN_GIANT_1
Bigfoot is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that appears in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], it is replaced with the [[Mutant Giant]].
## Spawning
Bigfoot appears in the [[Sadan]] fight arena in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. It drops down to attack the player after [[Sadan|Sadan's Interest Level]] has been emptied for the second phase of the fight. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], all four giants are replaced with [[Mutant Giant|Mutant Giants]].
## Behavior
Bigfoot will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They also stomp onto the ground every 7 seconds, dealing up to 5x the base [[Damage]] (88,000 ). The further away the player is, the less [[Damage]] they take.

## Trivia
- The Bigfoot has the same internal ID of SADAN_GIANT_1 as the other giants, including [[The Diamond Giant]], [[L.A.S.R.]], and the [[Jolly Pink Giant]].

## Spawning
Bigfoot has a 25% (1 in 4 spawns) chance to be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
Bigfoot will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They also stomp onto the ground every 7 seconds, dealing up to 5x the base [[Damage]] (57,600 ) in regular mode or 4x (607,500 [[Damage]]) it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]]. The further away the player is, the less [[Damage]] they take.

## Spawning
Bonzo can be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and above.
## Behavior
Bonzo will initially run towards the player to attack them with melee. Once they reach below 50% [[Health]], they will start repeatedly spin and firing balloons instead. These deal 0.6x the base [[Damage]] in regular mode, and 0.5x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].

# Chaos Guardian
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Chaos Guardian]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 185-487
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Arcane]] (M3 Only)
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000-120,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,880-54,000 (Base)
[Drops]
XP: 277-730 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 203-535
[Metadata]
ID: PROFESSOR_MAGE_GUARDIAN_1; MASTER_PROFESSOR_MAGE_GUARDIAN_1
The Chaos Guardian is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] during [[The Professor]]'s fight.
## Spawning
The Chaos Guardian spawns in one of the four containers of [[The Professor]]'s fighting arena. When killed, it will respawn 10 seconds later, unless the other three guardians ( [[Healthy Guardian]], [[Reinforced Guardian]], and [[Laser Guardian]]) have also been killed.
Each respawn reduces the max [[Health]] by 20% in normal mode and 10% in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], stacking multiplicatively between respawns. Additionally, in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], the respawn timer is reduced to 7 seconds.
## Behavior
### Lightning
The Chaos Guardian has an Anger Level above its head. This level is indicated by the amount of ⚡ lightning icons it has. This starts at level 1, and can reach level 6.
The level increases when hit enough times, with [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] requiring less hits to increase the level.
|Level Up|Hits Required Normal|Hits Required Master Mode|
|-|-|-|
|1 -> 2|6|4|
|2 -> 3|3|2|
|3 -> 4|3|2|
|4 -> 5|3|2|
|5 -> 6|3|2|
Every two seconds, the Chaos Guardian will strike lightning around itself, damaging players who are nearby. This deals 0.5x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode, or 0.7x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], multiplied by the Anger Level.
|Anger Level|Lightning Damage Normal|Lightning Damage Master Mode|
|-|-|-|
|1|1,440 [[Damage]]|37,800 [[Damage]]|
|2|2,880 [[Damage]]|75,600 [[Damage]]|
|3|4,320 [[Damage]]|113,400 [[Damage]]|
|4|5,760 [[Damage]]|151,200 [[Damage]]|
|5|7,200 [[Damage]]|189,000 [[Damage]]|
|Overcharge (12)|17,280 [[Damage]]|453,600 [[Damage]]|
Upon reaching Anger Level 6, the level will reset down to 1, and the guardian will prepare to overcharge. Several seconds later, lightning will rapidly strike in the surrounding area for several seconds, dealing the amount of [[Damage]] equivalent to an Anger Level of 12, or 17,280 in normal mode and 453,600 [[Damage]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
When the Chaos Guardian is killed and respawns, it keeps its Anger Level and internal hits counter until the next level. If it is killed during its overcharge buildup, it will not perform the attack.
### Body Slam
When the player gets too close for long enough, the Chaos Guardian will slam itself onto the ground, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (5,760 ), or 6x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (324,000 ).

|[Lv487]|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|120,000,000|54,000|730|535|162|MASTER_PROFESSOR_MAGE_GUARDIAN_1|

# Crypt Dreadlord
Aliases: Dreadlord
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Crypt Dreadlord]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 54-431
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 50%

[Drops]

[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_DREADLORD_#; MASTER_CRYPT_DREADLORD_#
The Crypt Dreadlord is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that shoots wither skulls at players with its sword. It drops the [[Dreadlord Sword]].
Like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], it can have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]]
## Spawning
The Crypt Dreadlord can spawn in normal rooms within [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] on any floor.
## Behavior
- It occasionally strafes to avoid attacks from the players.
- When the players are out of their reach they will either use the [[Dreadlord Sword|Dreadlord Sword's]] ability or shoot using a bow.

# Crypt Lurker
Aliases: Lurker
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Crypt Lurker]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 47-395
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 9,000-13,000,000; [[Damage]]: 291-72,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]]
XP: 70-592 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 51-434
[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_LURKER_#; MASTER_CRYPT_LURKER_#
Crypt Lurker is an enemy in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They very rarely drop Premium Flesh.
Like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], it can have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
## Behavior
Crypt Lurkers have a bone when they are spawned, and can throw that bone dealing massive damage. However, after they throw the bone, they act like normal zombies and are no longer threatening and can easily be defeated. The bone can also be easily dodged.

# Crypt Souleater
Aliases: Crypt Soulstealer, Souleater
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Crypt Souleater]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 53-429
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 50%
Spawn Condition: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) [[Wither]] [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 13,000-20,000,000; [[Damage]]: 327-99,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Soulstealer Bow]]
XP: 79-643 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 58-471
[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_SOULEATER_#; MASTER_CRYPT_SOULEATER_#
A Crypt Souleater is a mob that spawns in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
Its health, like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], scale on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
## Spawning
Crypt Souleaters can spawn within normal rooms in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] on any floor.
## Behavior
The Crypt Souleater will attempt to kill the player by firing wither skulls with a [[Soulstealer Bow]]. When in melee range, it will attempt to hit the player with a diamond sword.

# Crypt Undead
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Crypt Undead]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 89-220
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]) [[Mithril Deposits]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 45,000-800,000 (Base); [[Defense]]: 150; [[Damage]]: 936-11,700
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Undead Essence]] 0–1x [[Revive Stone]] 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]]
XP: 133-330 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 97-242
[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_UNDEAD_25; MASTER_CRYPT_UNDEAD_25
A Crypt Undead is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], as well as a [[Mob]] found in a specific room of the [[Mithril Deposits]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Crypt Undeads can only be spawned after blowing up a Crypt tomb in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] with [[Superboom TNT]], the [[Mage]] Guided Sheep ability, or the [[Conjuring]]'s ability. It can also be spawned in the [[Crystal Hollows]] if a [[Superboom TNT]] is used on a tomb in one of [[Boss Corleone]] spawning rooms.
## Behavior
Crypt Undeads attack by running towards the player. Although they wield a [[Bone]], they won't attack the player with it. It can also attack the player with a bow if you are far from it.
## Stats
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Defense]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|

|[Lv89]|The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]), [[Mithril Deposits]]|45,000|150|936|133|97|29|CRYPT_UNDEAD_25|
|[Lv220]|The Master Mode Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])|800,000|150|11,700|330|242|73|MASTER_CRYPT_UNDEAD_25|
When spawned in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], Crypt Undeads have a [[Health]] multiplier based on the floor they spawn in, using the following formula, rounding down:
Health=⌊BaseHealth×1.5(Floor−1)⌋
For [[The Catacombs - Entrance]], however, the [[Health]] is simply half of the base value, so 22,500 .
This results in the following [[Health]] values for each [[Catacombs|Floor]]:

# Deathmite
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Deathmite]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 772-864
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Trap Rooms)
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000; [[Damage]]: 800,000-3,000,000
[Drops]
XP: 1,158-1,296 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 849-950
[Metadata]
ID: DEATHMITE_3000; MASTER_DEATHMITE_3000
The Deathmite is an [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in trap rooms or from breaking a crypt in a specific room in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
Like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], it can have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
## Spawning
Multiple Deathmites can be found inside trap rooms in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. There is also a specific normal room with a crypt that spawns a Deathmite when it is broken.
## Behavior
Deathmite slowly crawl around and deal extremely high amounts of [[Damage]], instantly killing the player.

# Fairy
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mob pages missing internal IDs
[Fairy]
Type: [[Mob]]
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 4
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Revive Stone]] 0–1x [[Fairy Wings]]
Fairies are [[Dungeons]] mobs that spawn around [[Wilderness|Fairy Trees]], which will appear in pink rooms on the Dungeon Map. Fairies will drop a [[Revive Stone]], which can revive a dead player.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After teammate killed Fairy|<name> the Fairy: Have a great life!|
|Killing Fairy|<name> the Fairy: You killed me! Take this Revive Stone so my death is not in vain!|
|Killing Fairy whilst a Ghost|<name> the Fairy: You killed me! I will revive you so my death is not in vain!|
|Hitting with [[Shears]]|<name> the Fairy: I like your spirit, but those are not mysterious enough!|
|Hitting with [[Enchanted Shears]]|<name> the Fairy: You clipped my wings! Take this Fairy Wings so my loss is not in vain!|
## Trivia
- Fairies can have different names, including Akia, Tyene, Yve, Ilene or Steve.
- When [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] first released, the mechanics behind the Special Score calculation were unclear. This led to a popular misconception that killing Fairies reduced it, but this has since been proven to be false.
- Two Fairies will spawn in the stands during the [[Thorn]] boss fight.
- Contrary to what [[Tia the Fairy]] and the [[Fairylosopher|Fairylosophers]] say, they don't spawn a fairy soul upon death.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Fairies|
|Oct 5, 2022|Hitting a Fairy with [[Enchanted Shears]] now drops [[Fairy Wings]].|
|Sep 26, 2023|Mage Beam can now deal damage to Fairies.|

## Tips
- The [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]] are capable of hitting Fels.
- Berserks can also use the [[Flower of Truth]] or [[Bouquet of Lies]] to hit a Fels.
## Trivia
- Fels are likely connected to Fel Magic, which is described as magic capable of raising the dead. [[Jythar]] used it during The Calamity to preserve himself after death and split SkyBlock into floating islands; Jythar's heart was placed on an island, which corrupted it and transformed it into [[The End]]; Kaeman used it to transform into [[The Wither King]].
- When close to a Fels, it is possible to briefly see their nametag "Dinnerbone".
- Unlike normal Endermen, Fels can swim in water.

# Frozen Adventurer
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Frozen Adventurer]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 189-850
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Special Behavior: Uses the [[Ice Spray Wand]] to freeze players and quickly dishes out huge amounts of damage in a short time
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] Frozen ([[Frozen (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 285,000-160,000,000; [[Defense]]: 100; [[Damage]]: 958-180,000
[Drops]
Drops: 4–5x [[Ice Essence]] 1x [[Enchanted Ice]] 0–1x [[Hunk of Ice]] 0–1x [[Hunk of Blue Ice]] 0–1x [[Ice Spray Wand]] 0–1x [[Beating Heart]]
XP: 1,417-6,375 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 567-2550
[Metadata]
ID: LOST_ADVENTURER_#; MASTER_LOST_ADVENTURER_#
The Frozen Adventurer is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. It has lightning and freezing effects.
## Spawning
The [[Frozen Adventurer]] Frozen Adventurer spawns in normal or boss rooms within the [[Catacombs]] in the following floors:
- [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]
- [[The Catacombs - Floor V]]
- [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]
- [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
## Behavior
It acts similarly to a [[Lost Adventurer]], except it uses [[Frozen Blaze Armor]] and an [[Ice Spray Wand]] instead of [[Dragon Armor]] and the [[Aspect of the Dragons]]. It has a freezing aura about it that slows players within a short radius. Players within the radius are also struck by lightning. This lightning deals 10% of your maximum health in [[True Damage]].
In addition to attacking with a [[Swords|Sword]] and [[Bows|Bow]], it can also use the [[Ice Spray Wand]] on players, freezing them and amplifying the damage they take.
Similar to the [[Shadow Assassin]], this [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] deals high amounts of damage per second, and could kill the player instantly.

The Sleeping Golem / Woke Golem, also called the Golem in [[Bestiary]], is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] during the [[Sadan]] fight.
## Spawning
Multiple Sleeping Golems will appear past the starting hallway of the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Behavior
Sleeping Golems will turn into Woke Golems when initially attacked, causing them to quickly run towards players and attack them. They do not take any [[Damage]] on the first hit.

# Healthy Guardian
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Healthy Guardian]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 197-508
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000-120,000,000; [[Damage]]: 4,392-81,000 (Base)
[Drops]
XP: 295-762 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 216-558
[Metadata]
ID: PROFESSOR_PRIEST_GUARDIAN_1; MASTER_PROFESSOR_PRIEST_GUARDIAN_1
The Healthy Guardian is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] during [[The Professor]]'s fight.
## Spawning
The Healthy Guardian spawns in one of the four containers of [[The Professor]]'s fighting arena. When killed, it will respawn 10 seconds later, unless the other three guardians ( [[Healthy Guardian]] Healthy Guardian, [[Chaos Guardian]], and [[Reinforced Guardian]]) have also been killed.
Each respawn reduces the max [[Health]] by 20% in normal mode and 10% in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], stacking multiplicatively between respawns. Additionally, in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], the respawn timer is reduced to 7 seconds.
## Behavior
### Giga Heal
The Healthy Guardian will repeatedly complete the following cycle:
- Wait 60 seconds, or 45 seconds in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- Announce that a Giga Heal is about to occur, which will heal all of the alive guardians.
  - Stay in this idle phase for 10 seconds, or 8 seconds in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]]. This countdown timer is visible above the Healthy Guardian.
  - During this time, attacking the Healthy Guardian will reduce the healed amount by 5% per hit. Hitting 20 times will stop all healing from occurring.
  - The base amount healed is 10,000,000 [[Health]], or 210,000,000 [[Health]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
- If healing hasn't been disabled from being hit 20 times, heal all of the alive guardians.
- Start the cycle again.
### Body Slam
When the player gets too close for long enough, the Healthy Guardian will slam itself onto the ground, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (8,784 ), or 6x it in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (486,000 ).

# Jolly Pink Giant
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Jolly Pink Giant]

The Jolly Pink Giant is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that appears in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], it is replaced with the [[Mutant Giant]].
## Spawning
The Jolly Pink Giant appears in the [[Sadan]] fight arena in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. It drops down to attack the player after [[Sadan|Sadan's Interest Level]] has been emptied for the second phase of the fight. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], all four giants are replaced with [[Mutant Giant|Mutant Giants]].
## Behavior
The Jolly Pink Giant will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They can also occasionally throw a boulder towards the player when there's enough space, dealing 3x the base [[Damage]] (52,800 ). The further away the player is, the less [[Damage]] they take.

The Jolly Pink Giant, titled Giant in game, is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that can be summoned by [[The Watcher]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]. They also [[Jolly Pink Giant|appear]] in the [[Sadan]] fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Spawning
The Jolly Pink Giant has a 25% (1 in 4 spawns) chance to be spawned from [[The Watcher]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
The Jolly Pink Giant will run towards the player, damaging them when they get within range. They can also occasionally throw a boulder towards the player when there's enough space, dealing 5x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (57,600 ) and 3x it instead in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] (364,500 ).
